<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>he</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>成亲</rb><rt>dzing-tsʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>退婚了</rb><rt>tʽe-hweng-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>妨得个</rb><rt>fông-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有的个</rb><rt>yiu-tih-go</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Pi da-kæ eo* “*Yiæ-su kyüông-seng keh nyin*” *tsao ng nyin*. 比大概讴「<u>耶稣</u>降生箇年」早五年。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Yiæ-su *go ka-shih ziu-z* Kyiu-cü. <u>耶稣</u>个解说就是救主。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过个</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以马内列</rb><rt>YI-MÔ-NE-LIH</rt></ruby></u>；」 <ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>JING-MING</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>TENG</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>DÔ-KÔ</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>LÆ-TONG</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>调觉转来</rb><rt>diao-kao-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依了</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同床</rb><rt>dong-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头一个</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>。

---

### Mt. II.

<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>生出</rb><rt>Sang-cʽih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Pi da-kæ eo* “*Yiæ-su kyüông-seng keh nyin*” *tsao s nyin*. 比大概讴「<u>耶稣</u>降生箇年」早四年。（原注）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>召拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盘问</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>写落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>6</sup> 『<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>阙份</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>并非</rb><rt>bing-fi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>盘问</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-soh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>出现</rb><rt>Cʽih-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打听</rb><rt>tang-tʽing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奶花个</rb><rt>Na-hwun-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>打听着</rb><rt>tang-tʽing-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>国王个</rb><rt>koh-wông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>看见过个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-soh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>引导</rb><rt>ying-dao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉落了</rb><rt>deng-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>博老</rb><rt>*Poh-lao*</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>了弗得</rb><rt>liao-feh-teh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>𢳚开</rb><rt>hyiao-kʽæ</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>没药</rb><rt>mah-yiah</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*Keh-sing*</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>*poh-lao*</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>别埭</rb><rt>bih-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>归去</rb><rt>Kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连夜</rb><rt>lin-yia</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搬</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>临终</rb><rt>ling-cong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过个</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>讴出来了</rb><rt>eo-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>发怏</rb><rt>fah-ông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>博老</rb><rt>poh-lao</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>问出来个</rb><rt>meng-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>*zing-li*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>四乡</rb><rt>s-hyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>两岁</rb><rt>liang-shü</rt><rb>以下个</rb><rt>yi-ʽô-go</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>除灭掉</rb><rt>djü-mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利米</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉玛</rb><rt>Lah-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>号泪大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>悲伤个</rb><rt>pe-shông-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉结</rb><rt>Lah-kyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>呒得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>Siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>谋害</rb><rt>meo-ʽæ</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>Siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>Siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚基老</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>位</rb><rt>we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>梦</rb><rt>Mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>转弯</rb><rt>cün-w̆æn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Pi da-kæ eo* “*Yiæ-su kyüông-seng keh nyin*” *tsao sæn nyin*. 比大概讴「<u>耶稣</u>降生箇年」早三年。（原注）</sup>

---

### Mt. III.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>先知</rb><rt>Sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铺直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>街道</rb><rt>ka-dao</rt></ruby>，』」 <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>穿个</rb><rt>cʽün-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>系个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>缴身带</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>皮个</rb><rt>bi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蝗虫</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>ze-kyʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>毒蛇个</rb><rt>Doh-dzô-go</rt><rb>种党</rb><rt>cong-tông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>指引</rb><rt>ts-ying</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>将来个</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斧头</rb><rt>fu-deo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>斫倒</rb><rt>tsôh-tao</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>回心转意个</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>粪箕</rb><rt>feng-kyi</rt><rb>驮的</rb><rt>do-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>扬</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>晒场里个</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>仓里</rb><rt>tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>糠验</rb><rt>kʽông-nyiæn</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>执一</rb><rt>tsih-ih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>推辞</rb><rt>tʽe-dz</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>应许了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 27. 耶（稣）后（头）27（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>受过仔</rb><rt>ziu-ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>水个</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走上来</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>得意个</rb><rt>teh-i-go</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. IV.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>肚饥了</rb><rt>du-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>试试个</rb><rt>s-s-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>讲出来</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>圣城</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿个</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>屋脑尖</rb><rt>oh-nao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>Shü-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djôh</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>独门</rb><rt>doh-meng</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Mo-kwe. 就是魔鬼。（原注）</sup>

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>*lao-kæn*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>离开仔</rb><rt>li-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿个</rb><rt>pin-yin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>纳大利</u>个</rb><rt>Neh-da-li-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>西布伦</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>纳大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阴司</rb><rt>ing-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>遮暗</rb><rt>tsô-en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>住的个</rb><rt>djü-tih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（稣）后（头）30（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Y. ʽE. 31. 耶（稣）后（头）31（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>从此以后</rb><rt>Dzong-tsʽ-yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抲鱼个</rb><rt>kʽô-ng-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>Keng-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>个</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>补</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>症候</rb><rt>tsing-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>扬开了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>病症</rb><rt>bing-tsing</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>痴癫个</rb><rt>cʽü-tin-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>风瘫个</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>医好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>第加波利</rb><rt>Di-kyüô-po-li</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特买潮</rb><rt>deh-ma-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. V.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坐落仔</rb><rt>zo-lôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>穷个</rb><rt>gyüong-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>悲伤个</rb><rt>pe-shông-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>温柔个</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>依心满足</rb><rt>i-sing-mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>哀怜心</rb><rt>æ-lin-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>清洁个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>谎话</rb><rt>hwông-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>大勒紧</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天下个</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咸个</rb><rt>ʽæn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>呒用</rb><rt>m-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>倒勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>踏掉</rb><rt>dah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一座</rb><rt>Ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>建</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>看弗见个</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>斗桶下</rb><rt>teo-dong-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>*fông-leh*</rt><rb>灯台上</rb><rt>teng-dæ-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>毁掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>毁掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一点</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt><rb>一划</rb><rt>ih-wah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>废掉个</rb><rt>fi-diao-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>废掉个</rb><rt>fi-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>律例</rb><rt>lih-li</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一条</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>依从</rb><rt>*i-dzong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>*kong-yi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>无缘无故</rb><rt>vu-yün-vu-kwu</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉加</rb><rt>Lah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>魔利</rb><rt>Mo-li</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>地狱里个</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭坛前</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>冤恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭坛前</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zin</rt><rb>放间仔</rb><rt>fông-kæn-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>相和</rb><rt>siang-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>还完</rb><rt>wæn-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh tsia wô*, уiu kông peh kwu-z-tsin nying tʽing, z-ka wô. 或者话，有讲拨古时节人听是介话。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] \[3\] Lah-kyüô, *teng* Mo-li, *ziu-z Yiu-tʽa nying zôh-mô shih-wô*. <u>拉加</u>等<u>魔利</u>，就是<u>犹太</u>人𧮙骂说话。（原注）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>淫心</rb><rt>ying-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𫦉出</rb><rt>l̆eo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢掉仔</rb><rt>tiu-diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>地狱里</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斩落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢掉仔</rb><rt>tiu-diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>百体</rb><rt>pah-tʽi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>地狱里</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>离书</rb><rt>li-shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>离掉个</rb><rt>li-diao-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>罚咒个</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>许愿</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>还个</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>Ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>*vah-tsiu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>*tʽin*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>*vah-tsiu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>*di*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>踏脚凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>点点</rb><rt>tin-tin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>*vah-tsiu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>*Yiæ-lu-sah-leng*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>皇帝个</rb><rt>Wông-ti-go</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>黑</rb><rt>heh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做弗来</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>抵还</rb><rt>ti-wæn</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-*nying*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>刮</rb><rt>kwah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>巴掌</rb><rt>pô-công</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>刮</rb><rt>*kwah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>布衫</rb><rt>pu-sæn</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>袍子</rb><rt>bao-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>硬要</rb><rt>ngang-iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>一里</rb><rt>ih-li</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>Tʽao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>回复</rb><rt>we-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弄送</rb><rt>long-song</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天父个</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>收钱粮个</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>招接</rb><rt>tsiao-tsih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>收钱粮个</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>。
