### Mt. VI.

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>号头</rb><rt>ʽao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大街上</rb><rt>do-ka-zông</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>借只</rb><rt>tsia-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>暗洞洞个</rb><rt>en-dong-dong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大街上个</rb><rt>do-ka-zông-go</rt><rb>四岔路口</rb><rt>s-tsʽô-lu-kʽeo</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>Gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>密弗洞风</rb><rt>mih-feh-dong-fong</rt><rb>房里</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>关仔</rb><rt>kwæn-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要个</rb><rt>iao-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>晓得了</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>：——

「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>*long-tsong*</rt><rb>尊敬</rb><rt>tseng-kying</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>Sô-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>过失</rb><rt>ko-shih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>禁食个</rb><rt>kying-zih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忧闷个</rb><rt>iu-meng-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>变换</rb><rt>pin-wun</rt><rb>面色</rb><rt>min-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>㩤㩤</rb><rt>fu-fu</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>积聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蛀虫</rb><rt>cü-djong</rt><rb>蛀掉</rb><rt>cü-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发锈</rb><rt>fah-siu</rt><rb>霉烂</rb><rt>me-læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盗贼</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>偷去个</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>积聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蛀虫</rb><rt>cü-djong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>蛀掉</rb><rt>cü-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>发锈</rb><rt>fah-siu</rt><rb>霉烂</rb><rt>me-læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盗贼</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>拆</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>偷去</rb><rt>tʽeo-kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>肌身个</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>亮个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>暗个</rb><rt>en-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>暗个</rb><rt>en-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看轻</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>*zeo-meng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>性命</rb><rt>Sing-ming</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>*soh*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>*feh-z*</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>穿着</rb><rt>cʽün-tsiah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>*kwe-djong*</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>Tsʽia</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>秧</rb><rt>iang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>谷仓里</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>养</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一刻</rb><rt>ih-kʽeh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>愁</rb><rt>zeo</rt><rb>衣穿</rb><rt>i-cʽün</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>田里个</rb><rt>din-li-go</rt><rb>蟑螂</rb><rt>tsông-lông</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所罗门</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>勇个</rb><rt>üong-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>穿着</rb><rt>cʽün-tsiah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>及如</rb><rt>gyih-jü</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>蟑螂</rb><rt>tsông-lông</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>野草</rb><rt>yia-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火炉里</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>装扮</rb><rt>tsông-pæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>？<sup>32</sup> （<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>打算个</rb><rt>tang-sön-go</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt><rb>明朝个</rb><rt>ming-tsiao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>Ih-nyih</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>一日个</rb><rt>ih-nyih-go</rt><rb>劳苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt><rb>够了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. VII.

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>栋梁</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛里</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>挔出仔</rb><rt>i-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>栋梁</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>挔出</rb><rt>i-cʽih</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>眼睛里个</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li-go</rt><rb>栋梁</rb><rt>tong-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>挔出</rb><rt>i-cʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>眼睛里个</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>Tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>*nyi-cü*</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏掉仔</rb><rt>dah-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咬</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>捣</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讨个</rb><rt>tʽao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>寻个</rb><rt>zing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>捣</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-*kyü*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>包括进</rb><rt>pao-kweh-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>狭窄个</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>阔绰</rb><rt>kwʽeh-tsʽiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>宽大</rb><rt>kwʽun-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走进去个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>狭窄</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>及轧</rb><rt>gyih-gah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻着个</rb><rt>zing-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外头</rb><rt>*nga-deo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>凶恶个</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh-go</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>结个</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>紫葡萄</rb><rt>ts-bu-dao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>刺虎</rb><rt>tsʽ-hwu</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>摘</rb><rt>*tsah*</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>斫倒</rb><rt>tsôh-tao</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>Bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>*tseo-tsing-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>未来</rb><rt>mi-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>赶出过</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？』<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从来</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>落落来</rb><rt>lôh-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>江水</rb><rt>kông-shü</rt><rb>没上来</rb><rt>meh-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹过来</rb><rt>cʽü-ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>撞着</rb><rt>djông-djôh</rt><rb>箇进</rb><rt>keh-tsing</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>像脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好比像</rb><rt>hao-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>沙泥上</rb><rt>sô-nyi-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>落落来</rb><rt>lôh-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>江水</rb><rt>kông-shü</rt><rb>没上来</rb><rt>meh-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹过来</rb><rt>cʽü-ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>撞着</rb><rt>djông-djôh</rt><rb>箇进</rb><rt>keh-tsing</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>厉害个</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. VIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯个</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出来</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肯个</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>Da-mô-fong</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>Tön</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>福礼</rb><rt>foh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>风瘫</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>痛勒</rb><rt>tʽong-leh</rt><rb>苦弗过</rb><rt>kwʽu-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>舍边</rb><rt>sô-pin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢当</rb><rt>ken-tông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>好其</rb><rt>hao-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>跟间个</rb><rt>keng-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>碰见过</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>黑暗个</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>丈姆</rb><rt>dziang-m</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>潮热</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>潮热</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>生病个</rb><rt>sang-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>顶勒去</rb><rt>ting-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*Gyi-zi*</rt><rb>挑勒去</rb><rt>tʽiao-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特买潮</rb><rt>deh-ma-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>跟勒去</rb><rt>keng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>狐狸</rb><rt>Wu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>窠</rb><rt>kʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>庉身之</rb><rt>deng-sing-ts</rt><rb>处</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟勒去</rb><rt>keng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>海里个</rb><rt>Hæ-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>撼起来</rb><rt>hen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt><rb>盖住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>调觉</rb><rt>diao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒没了</rb><rt>tao-meh-de</rt></ruby>！」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>Ze-siu</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>太太平平了</rb><rt>tʽa-tʽa-bing-bing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革革沙</rb><rt>Keh-keh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>入魔</rb><rt>jih-mo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坟窠里</rb><rt>veng-kʽo-li</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>远点</rb><rt>Yün-tin</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>食</rb><rt>zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>队里</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>队里</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>山岩</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn</rt><rb>窜落</rb><rt>tsʽön-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浸煞了</rb><rt>tsing-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔勒去</rb><rt>peng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>入魔</rb><rt>jih-mo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>合</rb><rt>ʽEh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会会</rb><rt>we-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-dzing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>风瘫</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铺板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>困仔</rb><rt>kwʽeng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>风瘫</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赦罪个</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，」（<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>风瘫</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，）「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>铺板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>户粮房</rb><rt>wu-liang-vông</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起身</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>康健个</rb><rt>Kʽông-gyin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>医生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要个</rb><rt>*iao-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> 『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>福礼</rb><rt>foh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇节</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>*we-sing-cün-i*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>贺</rb><rt>ʽO</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>悲伤</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>将来个</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>禁食其</rb><rt>kying-zih-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>补</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>补的个</rb><rt>pu-tih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>绷碎</rb><rt>pang-se</rt><rb>箇件</rb><rt>keh-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>破绽</rb><rt>pʽo-dzæn</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>爆开</rb><rt>pao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弄环</rb><rt>long-wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>皮袋</rb><rt>bi-dæ</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>两样</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>保全了</rb><rt>pao-djün-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, ngang-sao-sao, m-neh ngô-kwông-go. 或者话，硬燥燥，呒得砑光个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>箇晌</rb><rt>keh-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死间了</rb><rt>si-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活其</rb><rt>weh-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>血漏</rb><rt>hyüih-leo</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦头</rb><rt>ken-den</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>单只消</rb><rt>tæn-tsih-siao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>Nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>管个</rb><rt>kwun-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>吹行</rb><rt>cʽü-ʽông</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>闹热彭生</rb><rt>nao-nyih-bang-sang</rt></ruby>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>走过仔</rb><rt>Tseo-ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>赶出仔</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>风声</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>扬开了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一进</rb><rt>ih-tsing</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开开了</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>千定</rb><rt>Tsʽin-ding</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>扬开了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>鬼王</rb><rt>kwe-wông</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>症候</rb><rt>tsing-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>脱力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>流落他乡</rb><rt>liu-lôh-tʽa-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>成管个</rb><rt>dzing-kwun-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>年成</rb><rt>Nyin-dzing</rt><rb>大熟</rb><rt>da-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做工个</rb><rt>tso-kong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, dziah-lih-de. 或者话，着力了。（原注）</sup>
