### Mt. X.

<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>症候</rb><rt>tsing-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>使徒个</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>个</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>；<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴多罗买</rb><rt>Pô-to-lo-mæ</rt></ruby></u>；<u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收钱粮个</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb><u>亚勒腓</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leh-fi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>勒拜</rb><rt>Leh-pæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>达太</rb><rt>Dah-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb><u>迦拿</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>失掉间个</rb><rt>shih-diao-kæn-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>i-lu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>病人</rb><rt>Bing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>得来个</rb><rt>teh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>缭缴里</rb><rt>liao-kyiao-li</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>缴</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>伙食</rb><rt>ho-zih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>双套</rb><rt>shông-tʽao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>哀杖</rb><rt>æ-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>*m-nao*</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>*ta*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃用</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-yüong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打听</rb><rt>tang-tʽing</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>谁拉</rb><rt>jü-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>可以个</rb><rt>kʽo-yi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忏念</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇份</rb><rt>Keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>忏念个</rb><rt>*tsʽæn-nyiæn-go*</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>掸掉</rb><rt>tæn-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脚里个</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>蛾摩拉</u>个</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*Peng-veng* *wô* be-bæn. 本文话备办。（原注）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>*tseo-tsing*</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>老实</rb><rt>lao-jih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>皇帝个</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>愁</rb><rt>zeo</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>讲法</rb><rt>kông-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>*pô*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>*song*</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>*bæn*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>*si-ze*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>en-dzông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>暗暗</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亮亮</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋瓦顶</rb><rt>oh-ngô-teng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>传开去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>麻雀</rb><rt>mô-tsiang</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>干戈</rb><rt>ken-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一杯</rb><rt>ih-pe</rt><rb>冷水</rb><rt>lang-*shü*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. XI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>吩咐好仔</rb><rt>feng-fu-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>IAH-ʽEN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聋𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：——

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>闲早</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹动个</rb><rt>cʽü-dong-go</rt><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>软相</rb><rt>nyün-siang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>软相</rb><rt>nyün-siang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>王宫</rb><rt>wông-kong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>Z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>*sin-cü-nying*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前头个</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出过</rb><rt>cʽih-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>强夺</rb><rt>gyiang-deh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>强个</rb><rt>gyiang-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>未来</rb><rt>mi-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>队伙</rb><rt>de-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>17</sup> 『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>笛子</rb><rt>dih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>悲伤</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>贪食</rb><rt>tʽen-zih</rt><rb>贪酒个</rb><rt>tʽen-tsiu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收钱粮个</rb><rt>Siu-zin-liang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人个</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>辨出</rb><rt>bin-cʽih</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>*Cü-kwu-go*</rt><rb>道义</rb><rt>dao-yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>做间</rb><rt>tso-kæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>21</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>可拉汛</rb><rt>Kʽo-lah-sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>必赛大</rb><rt>Pih-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>老早</rb><rt>lao-tsao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>麻衣</rb><rt>mô-i</rt><rb>遭勒</rb><rt>tsao-leh</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>回心转意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>西顿</u>个</rb><rt>Si-teng-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阴司里</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来东了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>才能个</rb><rt>dzæ-neng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>Væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>劳苦</rb><rt>lao-kʽwu</rt><rb>挑</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt><rb>重担</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>温柔</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu</rt><rb>谦虚个</rb><rt>kyʽin-hyü-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>轭</rb><rt>ah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活灵个</rb><rt>weh-ling-go</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>轭</rb><rt>ah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>容易个</rb><rt>yüong-yi-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>担头</rb><rt>tæn-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>轻个</rb><rt>kyʽing-go</rt></ruby>。」

### Mt. XII.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>麦田里</rb><rt>mah-din-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>摘勒</rb><rt>tsah-leh</rt><rb>麦佩</rb><rt>mah-be</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>喏</rb><rt>Nô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>摆间个</rb><rt>pa-kæn-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>律法里</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。——<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>*Ih-go*</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> 『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>福礼</rb><rt>foh-li</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒罪个</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日个</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>风了</rb><rt>fong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>*gyi-lah-go*</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁侬</rb><rt>jü-noh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地坑里</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>揸勒</rb><rt>cô-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>撩上来</rb><rt>liao-zông-læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>惊骇</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>鬼王</rb><rt>kwe-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup>
