<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>收拢</rb><rt>siu-long</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>散掉</rb><rt>sæn-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>赦弗掉个</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今世</rb><rt>kying-si</rt><rb>下世</rb><rt>ʽô-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赦弗掉</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>一株</rb><rt>Ih-cü</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>毒蛇个</rb><rt>Doh-dzô-go</rt><rb>种党</rb><rt>cong-tông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>溢出来个</rb><rt>kah-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>多头</rb><rt>to-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好人</rb><rt>Hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的个</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>*li-hyiang*</rt><rb>囥的个</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>虚话</rb><rt>hyü-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>账</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>奸淫个</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>鲸鱼</rb><rt>gying-ng</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>*Nyi-nyi-vi*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>Nen-pin-go</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>*nyü-wông*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所罗门</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所罗门</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> 「<ruby><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>Ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荒旱</rb><rt>hwông-ʽen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>』」<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>壁净清</rb><rt>pih-zing-tsʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扫</rb><rt>sao</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>装饰好了</rb><rt>tsông-sih-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>结煞个</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>坏了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>现在个</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>46</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>点点</rb><rt>tin-tin</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>！<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. XIII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海涂上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

「<ruby><rb>且听</rb><rt>TSʽIA-TʽING</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扎开去</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>飞来</rb><rt>fi-læ</rt><rb>吃掉了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>抽出来</rb><rt>tsʽiu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晒瘪</rb><rt>sa-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>枯落了</rb><rt>kwʽu-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>遮煞</rb><rt>tsô-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>结实</rb><rt>kyih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>*kyih*</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>奥妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有余</rb><rt>yiu-yü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听听</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt><rb>听弗进</rb><rt>tʽing-feh-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>应着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻其</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬了</rb><rt>ngang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>懒得</rb><rt>læn-teh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>闭的</rb><rt>pi-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>*foh-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>*iao-siang*</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>听闻过</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>扎的个</rb><rt>tsah-tih-go</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>暂时个</rb><rt>dzæn-z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逼难个</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>今世个</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物个</rb><rt>dzæ-veh-go</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒陶成了</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>结实</rb><rt>kyih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>困熟个</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主人家个</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>麦个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>抽苗</rb><rt>tsʽiu-miao</rt><rb>生佩个</rb><rt>sang-be-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看出了</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家个</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何里来</rb><rt>ʽah-li-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>？』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>Tsʽia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>两样</rb><rt>liang-yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收割个</rb><rt>Siu-keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把打把</rb><rt>pô-tang-pô</rt><rb>缚带拢</rb><rt>bo-tæn-long</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>烧</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>谷仓里</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>长起来</rb><rt>dziang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>菜蔬</rb><rt>tsʽæ-su</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>桠枝里</rb><rt>ô-ts-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>囥勒</rb><rt>kʽông-leh</rt><rb>三斗</rb><rt>sæn-teo</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>发酵</rb><rt>fah-kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>囥的个</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话出来</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>稗草个</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>话话</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>世界</rb><rt>shü-ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冤家</rb><rt>ün-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>稗草</rb><rt>bô-tsʽao</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火炉里</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光亮</rb><rt>kwông-liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, s-teh nying pæn-tih. 或者话，使得人𢲔跌。（原注）</sup>

<sup>44</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>田里</rb><rt>din-li</rt><rb>囥的个</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>横财</rb><rt>wang-dzæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>盖煞仔</rb><rt>kæ-sah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>值钿个</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>keh-lih</rt><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兜进</rb><rt>teo-tsing</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>*ng*</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖上</rb><rt>tʽo-zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>拣进</rb><rt>kæn-tsing</rt><rb>水桶里</rb><rt>shü-dong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt><rb>丢掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>时世</rb><rt>Z-shü</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火炉里</rb><rt>ho-lu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒牙齿</rb><rt>ngao-leh-ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>学会了</rb><rt>ʽôh-we-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>仓库</rb><rt>tsʽông-kwʽu</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>旧</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>驮出来</rb><rt>do-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>？<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>木匠</rb><rt>moh-ziang</rt><rb>师父个</rb><rt>s-vu-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>*feh-z*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约西</rb><rt>Iah-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>？<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>住东</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>呒体面个</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XIV.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>臣子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 32. 耶（稣）后（头）32（年）。（原注）</sup>

（<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希罗底</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>缚仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做生</rb><rt>tso-sang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希罗底</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>当面前</rb><rt>tông-min-zin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>行洗礼</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盘里</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>国王</rb><rt>Koh-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罚过咒</rb><rt>vah-ko-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>同席个</rb><rt>dong-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>盘里</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>*nyiang-ts*</rt><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。）

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>步行</rb><rt>bu-ʽang</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>迟了</rb><rt>dzi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>不必</rb><rt>peh-pih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>驮来</rb><rt>Do-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>忏念记</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>*feng*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>在外</rb><rt>dzæ-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*Zi*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>散开仔</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt><rb>荡来荡去</rb><rt>dông-læ-dông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>斗风</rb><rt>teo-fong</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>当夜</rb><rt>Tông-yia</rt><rb>四更</rb><rt>s-kang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走勒来</rb><rt>tseo-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放心</rb><rt>Fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>mang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>沉落去</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀声</rb><rt>wæ-sing</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>揸</rb><rt>cô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>疑心</rb><rt>nyi-sing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>息了</rb><rt>sih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>船里个</rb><rt>Jün-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革尼撒勒</rb><rt>Keh-nyi-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>*tʽong-cü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦头</rb><rt>ken-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>按个</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XV.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>；』 <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>Zôh-mô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>*de*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>！<u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>8</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>离远</rb><rt>li-yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>白力力</rb><rt>bah-lih-lih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>种的个</rb><rt>cong-tih-go</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>根</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>拔掉</rb><rt>bah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>瞎子个</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>窞里</rb><rt>den-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>话话</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>Lin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>！<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行落</rb><rt>ʽang-lôh</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>介出</rb><rt>ka-cʽih</rt><rb>坑子里</rb><rt>kʽang-ts-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>走出来个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>忖头</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫖</rb><rt>biao</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>盗贼</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趱</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>迦南个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>邪病</rb><rt>zia-bing</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>厉害个</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>怀怀</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>奉差</rb><rt>vong-tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>失掉间个</rb><rt>shih-diao-kæn-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿囡个</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>桌凳下</rb><rt>coh-teng-ʽô</rt><rb>跌落个</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心愿</rb><rt>sing-nyün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>残缺不全个</rb><rt>dzæn-kyʽüih-peh-djün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>*bing-nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>医好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>残缺个</rb><rt>dzæn-kyʽüih-go</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚个</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>饿勒</rb><rt>ngo-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>脱力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>何里来</rb><rt>ʽah-li-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇打</rb><rt>keh-tang</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>七个</rb><rt>Tsʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几梗</rb><rt>kyi-kwang</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谢好仔</rb><rt>zia-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>*feng*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>吃个</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>在外</rb><rt>dzæ-nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。
