### Mt. XVI.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夜快</rb><rt>Yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>*Yiu*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>红霞</rb><rt>ʽong-ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>风雨</rb><rt>fong-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>红霞</rb><rt>ʽong-ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>黑气</rb><rt>heh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>辨别</rb><rt>bin-pih</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>时世个</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>辨弗出</rb><rt>bin-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>奸淫个</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>箇岸</rb><rt>keh-ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忘记</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会为了</rb><rt>dza-we-we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>*ling-se*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>*ling-se*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>馒头个</rb><rt>mun-deo-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>*kwu-djôh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>Yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利米</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>巴约拿</rb><rt>Pô-Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>显出来个</rb><rt>*hyin-cʽih-læ-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阴司个</rb><rt>ing-s-go</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗过</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>天国个</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>钥匙</rb><rt>yiah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>缚住个</rb><rt>bo-djü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>释放个</rb><rt>sih-fông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Pe-teh *go* *ka-shih* *ziu-z*, zah-deo. <u>彼得</u>个解说就是石头。（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>从此以后</rb><rt>DZONG-TSʽ-YI-ʽEO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𠡒𠡒</rb><rt>te-te</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拦阻</rb><rt>læn-tsu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>好心</rb><rt>hao-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>*kwʽu-næn*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阻隔心</rb><rt>tsu-kah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>*liu-sing*</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撇掉</rb><rt>pʽih-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>*kʽeng*</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>遍天下个</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>*dzæ-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>国家个</rb><rt>koh-kô-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. XVII.

<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>高高</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>变出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>透光个</rb><rt>tʽeo-kwông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白个</rb><rt>bah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>现出东</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>三顶</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>雪刮亮个</rb><rt>shih-kwah-liang-go</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>盖住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>得意个</rb><rt>teh-i-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>*di-yiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>Tseo-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直等到</rb><rt>dzih-teng-tao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来过了</rb><rt>læ-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>聚拢间</rb><rt>*jü-long-kæn*</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>癫病</rb><rt>tin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>吃苦</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>*tih-lôh*</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>横绷勋流个</rb><rt>w̆ang-pang-hyüing-liu-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>趱</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>趱过去</rb><rt>tsæn-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做弗到个</rb><rt>tso-feh-tao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等门徒</rb><rt>teng-meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>忧闷了</rb><rt>iu-meng-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>地丁</rb><rt>di-ting</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>地丁</rb><rt>di-ting</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt></ruby>？」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>驮出个</rb><rt>Do-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>*kʽæ*</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>*kʽeo*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>谁拉</rb><rt>jü-lah</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>税钿</rb><rt>se-din</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>*wa-z*</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>儿囡个</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下落</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh</rt><rb>一枚</rb><rt>ih-me</rt><rb>钓鱼钩</rb><rt>tiao-ng-keo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>钓上来个</rb><rt>tiao-zông-læ-go</rt><rb>头一梗</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>挖开仔</rb><rt>w̆eh-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地丁</rb><rt>*di-ting*</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>*nying-ts*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Di-ting* *nying-ts*, *kôh-nying* *do-cʽih* *liang-din* *nying-ts*. 地丁银子，各人驮出两钿银子。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Keh-kwʽe* *nying-ts*, *yiu* *s-din* *djong*. 该块银子有四钿重。（原注）</sup>

---

### Mt. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>当中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>灵性折转</rb><rt>ling-sing-coh-cün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>低声小意</rb><rt>ti-sing-siao-i</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>磨上层</rb><rt>mo-zông-zeng</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>沉落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>深深</rb><rt>sing-sing</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斩落</rb><rt>tsæn-lôh</rt><rb>丢掉仔</rb><rt>tiu-diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>残疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𫦉出</rb><rt>l̆eo-cʽih</rt><rb>丢掉仔</rb><rt>tiu-diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>独眼</rb><rt>doh-ngæn</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>双眼</rb><rt>shông-ngæn</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>地狱个</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-go</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>殿上</rb><rt>tin-zông</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>失掉间个</rb><rt>shih-diao-kæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百只</rb><rt>ih-pah-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>迷路</rb><rt>mi-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>丢弃</rb><rt>tiu-kyʽi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>迷路个</rb><rt>mi-lu-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>迷路个</rb><rt>mi-lu-go</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>天父个</rb><rt>Tʽin-Vu-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一两个</rb><rt>ih-liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两三个</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠实了</rb><rt>kʽao-jih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>看待</rb><rt>kʽen-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>缚住个</rb><rt>bo-djü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>释放个</rb><rt>sih-fông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>合口齐动</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>回数</rb><rt>we-su</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>原谅</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七遭</rb><rt>Tsʽih-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七十个</rb><rt>Tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>账</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>债</rb><rt>tsa</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宽限</rb><rt>kwʽun-ʽæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>债</rb><rt>tsa</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>十两</rb><rt>jih-liang</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揸</rb><rt>cô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掐</rb><rt>kʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胡咙</rb><rt>wu-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>宽限</rb><rt>Kwʽun-ʽæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>*Yü-to*</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>恶</rb><rt>Ôh</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>债</rb><rt>tsa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发极</rb><rt>fah-gyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>皂吏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>*bæn*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>债</rb><rt>tsa</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>还完</rb><rt>wæn-wun</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>兄弟个</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>过短</rb><rt>ko-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天上个</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

### Mt. XIX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y ʽE. 33. 耶（稣）后（头）33（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>起初</rb><rt>Kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一男</rb><rt>ih-nen</rt><rb>一女</rb><rt>ih-nyü</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> 『<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>一体</rb><rt>ih-tʽi</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>一体</rb><rt>ih-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>离书</rb><rt>li-shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>离掉个</rb><rt>li-diao-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>Nen-nyüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>娘胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>太监</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>太监</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>太监</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>能够</rb><rt>Neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告记</rb><rt>tao-kao-kyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽAh-li</rt><rb>几条</rb><rt>kyi-diao</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>』，『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>』，『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>』，『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>谎告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>』，<sup>19</sup> 『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>』 ；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爱惜尔个</rb><rt>Æ-sih-ng-go</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。』 」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>顾着个</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到家</rb><rt>tao-kô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>全备</rb><rt>djün-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忧闷相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有佬</rb><rt>Yiu-lao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>天国里</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难勒紧</rb><rt>næn-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>Loh-do</rt><rb>穿过</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>针眼</rb><rt>tsing-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够个</rb><rt>neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>能够个</rb><rt>neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>弃掉仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-ts</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>重先</rb><rt>djong-sin</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>荣华个</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>在前个</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落后</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>在后个</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>。」
