### Mt. XX.

「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>雇</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>讲开</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>一钿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平直个</rb><rt>bing-dzih-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>净日</rb><rt>zing-nyih</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>雇</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>平直个</rb><rt>bing-dzih-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>园主</rb><rt>yün-cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>Eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>工人</rb><rt>kong-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>结煞个</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>头一伐</rb><rt>deo-ih-vah</rt><rb>上工个</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>上工个</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一钿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>头一伐</rb><rt>Deo-ih-vah</rt><rb>上工个</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一钿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>家主</rb><rt>kô-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>12</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>上工个</rb><rt>*zông-kong-go*</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>净日</rb><rt>*zing*-nyih</rt><rb>火热铁塌</rb><rt>ho-nyih-tʽih-tʽah</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！』<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>家主</rb><rt>Kô-cü</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讲开</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>一钿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>Ng-zi-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>注心</rb><rt>cü-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>结煞个</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在后个</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在前个</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落后</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>解去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>遭蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求个</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>能够个</rb><rt>Neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>预备好间个</rb><rt>yü-be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>压实</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>；<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>头个</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>讴勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXI.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯法基</rb><rt>Pah-fah-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对头个</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>雌驴</rb><rt>tsʽ-li</rt><rb>桩间</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驴</rb><rt>siao-li</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>解仔</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>牵来</rb><rt>kyʽin-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过了个</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>个</rb><rt>Shing-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>低声小意</rb><rt>ti-sing-siao-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>骑勒</rb><rt>gyi-leh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小驴</rb><rt>siao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。』」 

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>牵勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>雌驴</rb><rt>tsʽ-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小驴</rb><rt>siao-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>骑上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>铺</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>斫勒</rb><rt>tsôh-leh</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>铺</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>前后</rb><rt>Zin-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两潮</rb><rt>liang-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝赞个</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>\[1\] <u>希伯来</u>语 Hosanna，赞美神的用语</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>合城里</rb><rt>ʽeh-zing-li</rt><rb>人声</rb><rt>*nying-sing*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>摇动</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>Sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>买卖</rb><rt>ma-ma</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>推倒</rb><rt>tʽe-tao</rt><rb>兑</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>柜头</rb><rt>gyü-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>椅子</rb><rt>ü-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祷告个</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贼窠</rb><rt>zeh-kʽo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出来个</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>和散𠺶</rb><rt>ʽO-sæn-nah</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶花个</rb><rt>na-hwun-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>宿过</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>TSAO</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚饥了</rb><rt>du-kyi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>叶</rb><rt>yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>无花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>枯了</rb><rt>kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>无花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>枯勒</rb><rt>kwʽu-leh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>！」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>移过去</rb><rt>yi-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>投落</rb><rt>deo-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求个</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>？」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？』<sup>26</sup> <sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>Siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神国里</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>*sia-tih*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>起屋个</rb><rt>Kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落头</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>头一块</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>稀奇个</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-go</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌糊</rb><rt>tih-wu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>*keh-kwʽe*</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>*zah-deo*</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>压勒</rb><rt>ah-leh</rt><rb>粉碎</rb><rt>feng-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>Sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>*tsih-z*</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>天国</rb><rt>Tʽin-koh</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>酒饭</rb><rt>tsiu-væn</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>备好了</rb><rt>be-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>壮壮</rb><rt>công-công</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>杀好</rb><rt>sah-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>齐备了</rb><rt>zi-be-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走了去了</rb><rt>tseo-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>揸勒</rb><rt>cô-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遭蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发怏了</rb><rt>fah-ông-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>Hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>备好东</rb><rt>be-hao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四岔路口</rb><rt>s-tsʽô-lu-kʽeo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好好歹歹</rb><rt>hao-hao-tæ-tæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐满了</rb><rt>zo-mun-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>贺</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大衣</rb><rt>*do*-i</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>贺</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大衣</rb><rt>do-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>底下人</rb><rt>ti-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖出</rb><rt>tʽo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>黑暗个</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>装</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>箍套圈</rb><rt>kwʽu-tʽao-kyʽün</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇党</rb><rt>keh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>诚实个</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>实实惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>惧胁</rb><rt>gyü-hyih</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>恶意</rb><rt>ôh-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>投</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>通行钱</rb><rt>tʽong-ʽang-dzin</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>十块</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>番饼</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>头子</rb><rt>deo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国号</rb><rt>koh-ʽao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁侬个</rb><rt>jü-noh-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Kæ-sah *ziu-z* *Lo-mô* *wông-ti-go* *koh-ʽao*. <u>该撒</u>就是<u>罗马</u>皇帝个国号。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Keh-kwʽe* *fæn-ping* *dzih* *ih-din* *nying-ts* *go*: *kʽen*. Mt. 20. 该块番饼值一钿银子个。看 Mt. 20. 2（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[3\] *Peng-veng* *wô* z-ngæn. 本文话字眼。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>传宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>遗落</rb><rt>yi-lôh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>错了</rb><rt>tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>娶进</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>32</sup> 『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，』 <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死人个</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人个</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>36</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一条</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>精神</rb><rt>tsing-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>才情</rb><rt>dzæ-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <sup>40</sup> <sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢东个</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> 『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏脚凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

### Mt. XXIII.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>缚拢</rb><rt>bo-long</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>难挑个</rb><rt>næn-tʽiao-go</rt><rb>担头</rb><rt>tæn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一末</rb><rt>ih-meh</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>忠孝带</rb><rt>cong-hyiao-ta</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>阔阔个</rb><rt>kwʽeh-kwʽeh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦头</rb><rt>ken-den</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>大大个</rb><rt>do-do-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>酒筵上</rb><rt>tsiu-yin-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>头位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>上位</rb><rt>zông-we</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>fu-ts</rt></ruby>！

<sup>8</sup> <sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>锁住</rb><rt>so-djü</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>tʽin-koh-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>*meng*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸个</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戏头</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>漫长</rb><rt>mæn-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受罪</rb><rt>ziu-ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>水路</rb><rt>shü-*lu*</rt><rb>旱路</rb><rt>ʽen-*lu*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>入了</rb><rt>jih-leh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>地狱个</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>薄荷</rb><rt>bo-ho</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>茴香</rb><rt>we-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>芹菜</rb><rt>gying-tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>重头</rb><rt>djong-deo</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>信行</rb><rt>sing-ʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>错落了</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>荡样</rb><rt>dông-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇样</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>领路个</rb><rt>ling-lu-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>香子</rb><rt>hyiang-ts</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>滤出</rb><rt>li-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>loh-do</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>吞落去</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>碗盏</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt><rb>碟子</rb><rt>diah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>强横霸道</rb><rt>gyiang-wang-pô-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>猛心</rb><rt>mang-sing</rt><rb>𣥼满了个</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>瞎眼个</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>碗盏</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt><rb>碟子</rb><rt>diah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可比像</rb><rt>kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>粉白了个</rb><rt>feng-bah-liao-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好看个</rb><rt>hao-kʽen-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>尸骨</rb><rt>s-kweh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>𣥼满了个</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>假意</rb><rt>kô-i</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>塞满了个</rb><rt>seh-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>装饰</rb><rt>tsông-sih</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>恶贯</rb><rt>ôh-kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>dôh-dzô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>dôh-dzô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>地狱里个</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>？<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯</rb><rt>Üô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉迦</rb><rt>Pô-lah-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大殿个</rb><rt>*da*-din-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>杀掉个</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>应着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>回数</rb><rt>we-su</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鸡娘</rb><rt>kyi-nyiang</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yiah-sao</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>捂</rb><rt>u</rt><rb>小鸡</rb><rt>siao-kyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>！<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝赞个</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mt. XXIV.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>点点</rb><rt>tin-tin</rt><rb>圣殿个</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>连只</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>话话</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>冒勒</rb><rt>mao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。』<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>摇惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未了</rb><rt>mi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>时疫</rb><rt>z-yüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>*peh-ko*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>灾难个</rb><rt>tsæ-næn-go</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>刻忖</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup>
