<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>天国个</rb><rt>*tʽin*-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>国打国个</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但以理</rb><rt>Dæn-yi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>可恶个</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>读个</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>；）<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走落来</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大肚</rb><rt>do-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吃奶个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-na-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>冬天里</rb><rt>tong-tʽin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大难</rb><rt>da-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日脚</rb><rt>nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短点</rb><rt>tön-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>救弗来</rb><rt>kyiu-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日脚</rb><rt>nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短个</rb><rt>tön-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>密弗洞风</rb><rt>mih-feh-dong-fong</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>*læ-kæn*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>闪电</rb><rt>sin-din</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>亮过</rb><rt>liang-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老鹰</rb><rt>lao-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>灾难</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>发光</rb><rt>fah-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>跌落来</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>摇动</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>天下个</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>号筒个</rb><rt>ʽao-dong-go</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>四方</rb><rt>s-fông</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>嫩起来</rb><rt>neng-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叶爿</rb><rt>yih-bæn</rt><rb>爆出来</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>夏天</rb><rt>ʽô-tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>*koh*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>备好了个</rb><rt>be-hao-liao-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>*dong-nyü*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>锁好了</rb><rt>so-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>时辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ING-WE</rt><rb>天国</rb><rt>*tʽin-koh*</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>kʽo-pi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>武艺子</rb><rt>vu-nyih-ts</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>赚进</rb><rt>dzæn-tsing</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赚进</rb><rt>dzæn-tsing</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>窞</rb><rt>den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>囥间</rb><rt>kʽông-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>庉了</rb><rt>Deng-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>账</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>赚凑</rb><rt>dzæn-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忠心个</rb><rt>cong-sing-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>经手</rb><rt>kying-siu</rt><rb>有限个</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn-go</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>*kyi-jün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>经手</rb><rt>kying-siu</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>快乐个</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>赚凑</rb><rt>dzæn-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忠心个</rb><rt>cong-sing-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>经手</rb><rt>kying-siu</rt><rb>有限个</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn-go</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>*kyi-jün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>经手</rb><rt>kying-siu</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>快乐个</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>驮个</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>刻薄</rb><rt>kʽeh-boh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>种间</rb><rt>cong-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>割</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>扎间</rb><rt>tsah-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕了</rb><rt>pʽô-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>囥间</rb><rt>kʽông-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。』<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>懒惰个</rb><rt>læn-do-go</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>种间</rb><rt>cong-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>割</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>扎间</rb><rt>tsah-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>钱店里</rb><rt>dzin-tin-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>存间</rb><rt>dzeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自个</rb><rt>ngô-zi-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>*nying-ts*</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>利钿</rb><rt>li-din</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十千</rb><rt>jih-tsʽin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>有余</rb><rt>yiu-yü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>呒用个</rb><rt>m-yüong-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>黑暗个</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>荣华个</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>万国个</rb><rt>Væn-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>两号生</rb><rt>liang-ʽao-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>分出</rb><rt>feng-cʽih</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>祝福个</rb><rt>coh-foh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>备好东个</rb><rt>be-hao-tong-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>出门间</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>出膊个</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>？<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>出门间</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出膊个</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>？<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？』<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>*to-siao*</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>41</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒骂</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>备好间个</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>出门间</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>出膊个</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出门间</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>出膊个</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？』<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>*to-siao*</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>刑罚里</rb><rt>ying-vah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。』」
