### Mt. XXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该亚法</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>闹事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>贵</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>𣥼的个</rb><rt>tsi-tih-go</rt><rb>玉个</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>糜费掉</rb><rt>me-fi-diao</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>*dong-din*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>担</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>丧事</rb><rt>sông-z</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽEn-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>传开个</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>称</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>三十块</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从此之后</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ-ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>无酵节个</rb><rt>VU-KAO-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>某人</rb><rt>mo-nying</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>备好间</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>个打个</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>盆子里</rb><rt>beng-ts-li</rt><rb>下手个</rb><rt>ʽô-siu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>便宜</rb><rt>bin-i</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝谢仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>新约个</rb><rt>sing-iah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>国里</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\]*Yiu-teh* *Hyi-lah* *kwu-kyün* *wô*, *coh-zia-ts*: *kʽen* Mk. 6. 41. 有得<u>希腊</u>古卷话，祝谢仔。看 Mk. 6. 41.（原注）</sup>

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>圣诗</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>唱好</rb><rt>tsʽông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>31</sup> 「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>Kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>Kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, tsæn-me-s. 或者话，赞美诗。（原注）</sup>

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>客西马尼</rb><rt>Kʽeh-si-mô-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>等东</rb><rt>teng-tong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西庇太</u>个</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa-go</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>悲伤起来了</rb><rt>pe-shông-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好使得</rb><rt>hao-s-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>灵性</rb><rt>Ling-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>软弱</rb><rt>nyün-ziah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>Di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>使弗得个</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>Tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>眯拢去了</rb><rt>mi-long-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话法</rb><rt>wô-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>困熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将息</rb><rt>tsiang-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>罪人个</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>GYI</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉个</rb><rt>diao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>暗号</rb><rt>en-ʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴个</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>Ze-siu</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>刀壳里</rb><rt>tao-kʽôh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斩个</rb><rt>*tsæn-go*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斩煞</rb><rt>tsæn-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>营</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>应勒来</rb><rt>ing-leh-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>书里个</rb><rt>shü-li-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>逃走了</rb><rt>dao-tseo-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>Kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该亚法</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>*ngô-meng-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>聚拢间</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>结局</rb><rt>kyih-gyüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>毁掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>63</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>64</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>别人个</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>67</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拳头</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>掴</rb><rt>kwah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>68</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>讲讲</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh-tsia* *wô*, pin-ts tang-Gyi. 或者话，鞭子打其。（原注）</sup>

<sup>69</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明堂里</rb><rt>ming-dông-li</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>*ô-deo*</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>72</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>73</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做队</rb><rt>tso-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>辨勒</rb><rt>bin-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>74</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>甚大咒</rb><rt>zing-do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>75</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲过个</rb><rt>kông-ko-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXVII.

<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>缚仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt><rb>带出去</rb><rt>ta-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本丢</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>定实</rb><rt>ding-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三十块</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪过</rb><rt>ze-ko</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>与</rb><rt>Yü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>承当</rb><rt>dzing-tông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>*sing*-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吊煞了</rb><rt>tiao-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>卖血个</rb><rt>ma-hyüih-go</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>银库里</rb><rt>nying-kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>烧窑个</rb><rt>siao-yiao-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>出门个</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>血田</rb><rt>Hyüih-din</rt></ruby>」。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利米</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三十块</rb><rt>sæn-jih-kʽwe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>市值估价</rb><rt>z-dzih-kwu-kô</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>公估个</rb><rt>kong-kwu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮出去</rb><rt>do-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>烧窑个</rb><rt>siao-yiao-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>节肯个</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>如</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有名个</rb><rt>yiu-ming-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗过</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上来个</rb><rt>zông-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>堂</rb><rt>dông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管账</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>撺掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>讴勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒设法</rb><rt>m-shih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>反响</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>闹事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>流出</rb><rt>*Liu-cʽih*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>承当</rb><rt>dzing-tông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>总督个</rb><rt>tsong-toh-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官厅里</rb><rt>kwun-tʽing-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>合营个</rb><rt>ʽeh-ying-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>*cʽü-siao*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>编出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇根</rb><rt>keh-keng</rt><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>pe</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>TSEO</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>各各他</rb><rt>Kôh-kôh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>骷颅头</rb><rt>kwʽu-lu-deo</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>苦胆</rb><rt>kwʽu-tæn</rt><rb>和的个</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>尝着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>。」
