<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>贴</rb><rt>tʽiah</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>*ih-da*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>条子</rb><rt>*diao-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>走过个</rb><rt>Tseo-ko-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>摇摇</rb><rt>yiao-yiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>40</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>毁掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>*sing*-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起好</rb><rt>kyʽi-hao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走落来</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>嘲笑</rb><rt>tsao-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>救过了</rb><rt>kyiu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>钉间个</rb><rt>ting-kæn-go</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>Mao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>以利</rb><rt>Yi-li</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>拉马</rb><rt>lah-mô</rt><rb>撒白大尼</rb><rt>sah-bah-da-nyi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>立间个</rb><rt>lih-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>奔起</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>海绵花</rb><rt>hæ-min-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醋</rb><rt>tsʽu</rt><rb>渗透仔</rb><rt>seng-tʽeo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳</rb><rt>cʽoh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>芦竿里</rb><rt>lu-ken-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮了</rb><rt>Vong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>断了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>圣殿里个</rb><rt>*Sing*-din-li-go</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>上下</rb><rt>zông-ʽô</rt><rb>𠡒裂开</rb><rt>te-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>震动</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>裂开</rb><rt>lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt><rb>圣人个</rb><rt>sing-nying-go</rt><rb>魄尸</rb><rt>pʽoh-s</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坟里</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣城里</rb><rt>sing-dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>*bih-nyiang-kao*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕起来</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看间</rb><rt>kʽen-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约西</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-si-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>TAO</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚利马太</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>59</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>干净个</rb><rt>ken-zing-go</rt><rb>细</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>裹好仔</rb><rt>ko-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>石岩</rb><rt>zah-ngæn</rt><rb>凿出来</rb><rt>zôh-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>寿坟里</rb><rt>ziu-veng-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>翻</rb><rt>fæn</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>塞</rb><rt>seh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>TAO</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>预备日个</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih-go</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>63</sup> 「<ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>Da-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>骗贼</rb><rt>pʽin-zeh</rt><rb>活前</rb><rt>weh-zin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>夜头</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>后头个</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo-go</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>65</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一队</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>武艺子</rb><rt>vu-nyih-ts</rt><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>*veng*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>封皮</rb><rt>fong-bi</rt><rb>封好</rb><rt>fong-hao</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇队</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>管间</rb><rt>kwun-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>牢靠</rb><rt>lao-kʽao</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mt. XXVIII.

<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>七日个</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>震动过了</rb><rt>cing-dong-ko-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻过</rb><rt>fæn-ko</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>*veng*</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>闪电</rb><rt>sin-din</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>*liang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>管间个</rb><rt>Kwun-kæn-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>*ping*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吓勒</rb><rt>hah-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>葬个</rb><rt>tsông-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔去</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt><rb>通报</rb><rt>tʽong-pao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>通报</rb><rt>tʽong-pao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇队</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>通报</rb><rt>tʽong-pao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>商量好</rb><rt>siang-liang-hao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>瞌𥅻</rb><rt>kʽeh-cʽong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>偷勒去了</rb><rt>tʽeo-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>传去</rb><rt>djün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>十一</rb><rt>JIH-IH</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一座</rb><rt>*ih-zo*</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话过个</rb><rt>wô-ko-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>Tʽin-zông</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>招</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>万国个</rb><rt>væn-koh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
